# September 12, 2026 — American Fork PD Car-to-Car transcript

Production archive: [AFPD police radio](https://afpd-accountability.com/police-radio/). Talkgroup **6001**; **991 clips**, in archive order.

All clock timestamps are **Mountain Daylight Time (America/Denver, UTC−06:00)**. Durations are the archive’s rounded displayed clip lengths; their sum is **52 minutes 34 seconds**.

**Review draft.** Each clip was processed from its original audio and a filtered version, and checked in an adjacent-call context pass using Whisper small.en. All three recognition outputs were compared and reconciled; this was ASR-output review, not direct human listening. Brackets mark uncertain words, alternative readings, or unclear transmissions. Obvious spelling and transcription errors were normalized; uncertainty was retained for names, codes, numbers, and wording that could change meaning. Speaker identities were not inferred.

Each numbered line contains the clip’s clock timestamp, displayed length, a link to its original audio file, and the transcript.
